- Checks: `npm run build`, `npm run check:bundle`, `npm run typecheck:functions` and `npm run test:all` pass (unit 1395 in 61 files, up from 1352 in 59; integration 725 in 55 files, up from 674 in 54). `npm run lint` is 34 errors and 4 warnings before and after; no touched file has an error. `npx wrangler pages functions build` compiles. `scanner:check` was not run; nothing under `scanner/` or `functions/utils/scan/` changed. Nothing was run against a remote database.

### Step 15: no member pricing, and the LCA membership requirement

- **One price.** `priceEntry(section, tournament, nowMs)` and `priceShownSection(section, tournament, nowMs)` in `domain/registration/pricing.ts` lost their fourth argument. The "LCA member discount" line and `PriceTournament.member_discount` are gone. Regular, early and late come from the section row and the live tournament columns as before, so a member and a non-member entering the same section at the same moment pay the same in every tier, even when `member_discount` is above 0 in the row. `functions/api/registrations.ts`, `registrations/batch.ts` and `admin/tournaments/[id]/waitlist.ts` price without member status. `registrations/[id].ts` never used the member discount and did not change. Their SQL did not change, so under the Drizzle conversion rule nothing converted; the `membership_status` selects in `batch.ts` and `waitlist.ts` stay, unused.
- **Migration `0054_requires_lca_membership.sql`**, generated with `npm run db:generate -- requires lca membership`: `ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD requires_lca_membership integer DEFAULT 1 NOT NULL`. After a statement breakpoint, one data statement added by hand, as the header says: `UPDATE tournaments SET requires_lca_membership = 0 WHERE club_id IS NOT NULL AND requires_lca_membership <> 0`, so existing club-run events start with the requirement off. A second run changes nothing, and the header warns that it must not be run by hand once a club has switched the requirement on (wrangler runs each migration once). The update fires no trigger: the only update trigger on `tournaments` watches `sections` and `round_schedule`. `member_discount` stays in the table, unread and no longer written, so no data is dropped. `drizzle/meta/_journal.json` has its fourth entry and `drizzle/meta/0003_snapshot.json` differs from the previous snapshot only by the new column. `migrations/README.md` says `0055` is next and gains a short section on adding a data statement after a generated change, with 0054 as the example. `drizzle-kit generate` afterwards reports "No schema changes, nothing to migrate". 0054 was applied to the local D1 only (`wrangler d1 migrations apply lca-db --local`).
- **The rule.** `domain/membership/requirement.ts` (no zod, no imports): `requiresLcaMembership(tournament, { forceAll? })` is true for an LCA-run event (`club_id` null) whatever the column says, otherwise the column, or true when `FORCE_LCA_MEMBERSHIP` is on; `hasActiveMembershipOn(player, date)`; `needsMembershipAtCheckout(player, tournament)`, true when the event requires it and the player has no active membership on the event date, for WS06 to call; and `LCA_RUN_NEEDS_MEMBERSHIP`, the plain 400 message. `FORCE_LCA_MEMBERSHIP` is a constant, off today. Brief decision D12 plans an admin-only site setting `membership_required_for_all`, built with the site settings in WS08, that replaces the constant; the module's comment says so.
- **Who may set it** (decision 5 as applied). On `PATCH /api/admin/tournaments/[id]`, a body carrying `requiresLcaMembership` is accepted from an LCA admin, or from a club rep whose club is the event's club. An assigned director, who manages the event through `canManageTournament` but is neither, gets 403, even when sending the value the event already has; so do another club's rep (assigned or not), a rep assigned to an LCA-run event and a plain member. All checks run before any write. Sending false when the event will have no club after the save is 400 ("An LCA membership is always required for an event LCA runs. Only a club-run event can switch it off."). Taking the club off an event forces the column to 1, and every save of an event with no club writes 1. Otherwise the column is written only when sent, so a stale edit cannot undo another save's change. `POST /api/admin/tournaments` stores 0 for a new event with a club and 1 otherwise, unless the body says otherwise, and the false-with-no-club case is 400. Both writes go into the existing Drizzle `set()` and `insert`. Both endpoints accept `memberDiscount` and drop it, so the current setup form and older clients keep working.
- **Contracts.** `domain/contracts/events.ts`: the tournament row schema gains `requires_lca_membership` (so the list, detail, manage and admin answers carry it); the create and edit requests gain `requiresLcaMembership: z.boolean().optional()`; `memberDiscount` stays in the edit request, marked as accepted and ignored. `contracts-pending.ts` is unchanged (no new route). `functions/types.ts`: `TournamentRow.requires_lca_membership`; `functions/db/schema.ts` gains the column and a "retired" comment on `memberDiscount`. `src/lib/api.ts` drops `memberDiscount` and gains an optional `requiresLcaMembership` on the create and update bodies.
- **Pages.** The member-discount input is gone from `TournamentManagePage.tsx` (state, loading and sending), the discount grid goes from three columns to two, and the label "Discounts and late fee" reads "Early-entry discount and late fee". `TournamentDetailPage.tsx` drops the "LCA members save $X" line, the member price line, `isLcaMember` and the `selfIsLcaMember` prop. `FamilyRegistrationPanel.tsx` prices each player by section only (`Player.isLcaMember` and the `selfIsLcaMember` prop are gone). `HomePage.tsx` no longer lists "discounted tournament entry" as a membership benefit; it reads "entry to LCA-run tournaments across the state". These removals ship without a switch (see Deviations).
- **The setup switch.** A checkbox on the Registration tab, "Players need a current LCA membership to enter", with its state in words below ("Required: ..." or "Not required: ..."), behind the new switch `membershipRequirementSwitch` in `src/lib/features.ts` (off). It is drawn only when the switch is on, the event has a club, and the viewer is an LCA admin or the rep of that club. The page sends `requiresLcaMembership` only when its value changed, so an assigned director saving other settings never meets the 403. **Leave `membershipRequirementSwitch` off until WS06 enforces the rule at checkout**: the state text says every player must hold a membership, and until then a non-member can still enter. The flag's comment in `src/lib/features.ts` says the same.
- **Every tournament answer gives `member_discount` 0 (checkpoint B review).** `toTournamentResponse` in `functions/utils/events/sectionsRepo.ts` answers `member_discount: 0` for every tournament, whatever the stored column holds, which stays unchanged. Why: browser tabs opened before the deploy still run the old site code, which subtracts the answered member discount from a member's price. With the real value in the answer, those tabs would keep showing the member price while Stripe charges the one price. With 0 they show what Stripe charges. Every answer that carries a whole tournament row goes through this function (public list and detail, admin manage, create, edit and registration edit); the other answers select named columns without it, and registration, batch registration and waitlist read the row only inside. The contract still passes, since 0 is a valid dollar amount. `test/unit/no-member-price-surface.test.ts` now allows exactly that one line in `sectionsRepo.ts`, and `test/integration/pricing-no-member-discount.test.ts` shows an event stored with 5 answering 0 on the public detail (signed out and signed in), the public list and admin manage, with the column still 5. The same review fixed the frozen price time (see Deviations, step 13).
- **Not done here.** Nothing refuses or charges a non-member at checkout, because today's registration has no way to add a membership inline and refusing would leave non-members with no way forward. That, and the schedule picker, are WS06's. The full setup toggle with its fee ladder is WS08's.
- **Before checkpoint B merges.**
  - K runs `SELECT id, name, member_discount FROM tournaments WHERE member_discount > 0 AND status != 'completed'` on production (`npx wrangler d1 execute lca-db --remote --command "<query>"`). **Result (Monday, October 12, 2026): no rows.** An open event with a member discount would change price mid-registration when this step deploys, because the server-side price change cannot sit behind a frontend switch.
  - Migrations 0052 and 0053 are on production. `migrate-db.yml` applied them on Friday, October 9, 2026 at 12:28 PM UTC, on its fifth attempt after the GitHub Actions token was given access to the LCA account.
  - 0054 must reach production before the deploy of this step, or with it. The event edit reads `requires_lca_membership` and the event create writes it, so without 0054 in production every event edit and every event creation would answer 500. `migrate-db.yml` applies it on the merge to `main`, in seconds, while the Pages build takes minutes. If that run fails, the merge is not finished until it is re-run and passes.
- Tests: new `membership-requirement.test.ts`, `no-member-price-copy.test.ts`, `no-member-price-surface.test.ts`, `migration-0054-requirement.test.ts`, `tournament-manage-no-member-discount.test.tsx` and `tournament-manage-membership-switch.test.tsx` (unit); `pricing-no-member-discount.test.ts`, `requires-membership.test.ts` and `requires-membership-edges.test.ts` (integration). Updated: `pricing.test.ts` (216 cases, down from 432, because the member variants went), `registration-rules.test.ts` ("the member and the guest both pay 20", both with `amountCents` 2000), `tournament-pages-pricing.test.tsx`, `registration-section-reads.test.ts`, `contracts/walkins-waitlist.test.ts`, `contracts/walkins-waitlist-edges.test.ts`, `domain-shared-rules.test.ts`, `sections-client-readers.test.ts` and `check-bundle.test.ts`. Pins moved: `schema-drift.test.ts` 313 to 314 columns, `contracts-layer.test.ts` 44 to 45 keys, `drizzle-baseline.test.ts` 55 files with 0054 last, `db-generate-guard.test.ts` (0055 next, snapshots up to 0004), `db-generate-real-kit.test.ts` (four journal entries, sandbox files named `0055_*`) and the fixture `drop-quoted-parent.sql`.
- Checks: `npm run build`, `npm run check:bundle` (no zod in 115 files), `npm run typecheck:functions` and `npm run test:all` pass (unit 1447 in 67 files, up from 1395 in 61; integration 787 in 58 files, up from 725 in 55). `npm run lint` is 34 errors and 4 warnings before and after; no touched file has an error (`HomePage.tsx` keeps its existing `exhaustive-deps` warning). `npx wrangler pages functions build` compiles. `scanner:check` was not run; nothing under `scanner/` or `functions/utils/scan/` changed. Nothing was run against a remote database.

### Checkpoint B: steps 9 to 15

**Merged into `main` as `748a5ef` on Monday, October 12, 2026 at 10:29 PM UTC** (pull request 104, a merge commit on head `b41ac45`). `migrate-db.yml` applied 0054 to production at 10:29:51 PM UTC on its first attempt; `d1_migrations` now ends at `0054_requires_lca_membership.sql`. The Pages production build and the daily-emails worker deploy both succeeded. The post-deploy query for club events with the requirement on returned no rows (production has no club-run event), and the one LCA-run event has it on. The public site answered afterwards; its only event is hidden, so there was no public row to inspect. Checkpoint B moves tournament sections and schedules off their JSON columns and onto the tables checkpoint A created, then removes member pricing.

- **Step 9:** one writer for sections (`sectionsRepo.ts`), rename by id, the refusal to remove a section that has entries or games, 409 when a save raced another, and the first Drizzle use in the admin tournament create and edit.
- **Step 10:** player registration (single, family and entry edit) reads the section rows, sets `section_id` itself, refuses archived sections, and answers malformed bodies with `{ error, fields }`.
- **Step 11:** walk-ins and waitlist offers read the section rows.
- **Step 12:** every server answer that carries a tournament reads sections from the table and gains `id`, `cap` and `fees`; `round_schedule` is answered as a list.
- **Step 13:** the pages read the contract types, price through `priceShownSection`, and `npm run check:bundle` keeps zod out of the site.
- **Step 14:** one writer and one reader for schedules, `schedules[]` in every tournament answer, and new 400 and 409 refusals for schedule edits.
- **Step 15:** no member price anywhere, `requires_lca_membership` (migration 0054) and who may set it.
- **Migrations in this checkpoint:** 0054 only (0052 and 0053 belong to checkpoint A). It adds one column and one idempotent update, and drops nothing.
- **Visible to people without a switch:**
  - One price for everyone: an active LCA member pays the same as a non-member in every section and tier (single entry, family entry and waitlist offers). On an event with a member discount, a member's Stripe charge goes up by that discount. Non-member amounts are unchanged.
  - The event page no longer shows "LCA members save $X on entry. Join LCA" or the "LCA member discount" line in the price box and the confirmation dialog. The section list and the family panel show one price per section.
  - The setup page (Registration tab) no longer has the "LCA member discount ($)" box. The label "Discounts and late fee" reads "Early-entry discount and late fee", with two boxes to a row.
  - The home page membership blurb "including discounted tournament entry, your official membership profile, and access to events across the state" now reads "including entry to LCA-run tournaments across the state and your official membership profile".
  - New plain refusals when editing an event: a section with entries or paired games cannot be removed; two sections cannot share a name; schedule edits have new refusals; simultaneous saves get "These sections changed while you were saving. Reload the page and try again."
  - The US Chess rating report includes players entered under a section later removed; its messages say "US Chess" and show weekday dates; an event whose report downloaded cleanly can be blocked until those players have US Chess IDs.
  - Registration and walk-in refusals say "US Chess ID"; the membership-expiry warning shows the weekday (for example "Tue, Sep 1, 2026"); a badly formed request gets "Some fields are missing or not valid."
  - A section saved without a fee of its own (very old events only) now shows the event fee instead of "Free", and the event fee is written into it the first time its sections are saved. No price changes.
  - A "Players need a current LCA membership to enter" switch exists on the setup page, hidden behind `membershipRequirementSwitch` (off). Nothing checks membership at checkout yet.
  - Not unchanged for a visitor, so the old claim that everything else is unchanged is dropped. Remaining wording that still says USCF on the event page and in the family panel is listed under Follow-ups.
- **Gate after the checkpoint B review fixes:** lint 34 errors and 4 warnings (the baseline was 37 and 4, and no step raised it; every file touched in the review lints clean), build, `check:bundle` (no zod in 115 files), `typecheck:functions`, unit 1457 in 67 files and integration 790 in 58 files all pass. At the end of step 15 the counts were unit 1447 and integration 787. `scanner:check` was not run; nothing under `scanner/` or `functions/utils/scan/` changed.
- **Before the merge: every query below was run on production on Monday, October 12, 2026** (`npx wrangler d1 execute lca-db --remote --command "<query>"`, read-only, one statement per command, from the cloud session with the LCA account's token). Production then held 1 tournament ("Test Tournament Name Sept 2026", status active, no club) and 15 entries. Results: the member-discount query returned no rows; the four step 8 queries each returned 0; every legacy-shape query returned no rows. Nothing blocks the merge on the data.
  1. The member-discount query. A row means an open event has a member discount. After the deploy a member pays full price, and tabs still running the old code would keep showing the member price (the server now answers 0, see Step 15, so they show the true price). K accepts that before merging.

     ```sql
     SELECT id, name, member_discount FROM tournaments WHERE member_discount > 0 AND status != 'completed';
     ```
  2. The four step 8 queries again (0052 and 0053 are applied). Each expects 0.
  3. Legacy-shape checks, each expected to return no rows.

     Saves that would now be refused (a section name over 80 characters or blank, a negative fee, prizes stored as a list, or a custom detail that is not a title and body). A row is an event whose next save would be locked out with the generic message:

     ```sql
     SELECT tournament_id, name FROM tournament_sections WHERE archived_at IS NULL AND (length(name) > 80 OR trim(name) = '' OR fee_regular < 0 OR json_type(prizes_json) = 'array');
     SELECT t.id FROM tournaments t, json_each(CASE WHEN json_valid(t.custom_details) AND json_type(t.custom_details)='array' THEN t.custom_details END) e WHERE e.type <> 'object' OR json_type(e.value,'$.title') IS NOT 'text' OR json_type(e.value,'$.body') IS NOT 'text';
     ```

     Price or display changed by wrong-typed keys. A row is a section whose stored extra keys could change its price, prize fund, rules or rating limits on the new pages:

     ```sql
     SELECT tournament_id, name, extra_json FROM tournament_sections WHERE archived_at IS NULL AND json_valid(extra_json) AND (json_type(extra_json,'$.entryFee') IS NOT NULL OR json_type(extra_json,'$.prizeFund') IS NOT NULL OR json_type(extra_json,'$.rulesSet') IS NOT NULL OR json_type(extra_json,'$.unratedOk') IS NOT NULL OR json_type(extra_json,'$.ratingMax') IS NOT NULL OR json_type(extra_json,'$.ratingMin') IS NOT NULL);
     ```

     "Free" becomes the event fee. A row is a section with no fee of its own on an event that has a fee, which will now show and charge the event fee:

     ```sql
     SELECT s.tournament_id, s.name, t.entry_fee FROM tournament_sections s JOIN tournaments t ON t.id = s.tournament_id WHERE s.archived_at IS NULL AND s.fee_regular IS NULL AND t.entry_fee > 0;
     ```

     Waitlist and move repricing for removed sections. A row is a removed section that still has live entries and a fee, whose waitlist offers or moves would be priced from it:

     ```sql
     SELECT s.tournament_id, s.name, s.fee_regular, COUNT(*) FROM tournament_sections s JOIN registrations r ON r.section_id = s.id WHERE s.archived_at IS NOT NULL AND s.fee_regular IS NOT NULL AND r.withdrawn_at IS NULL GROUP BY s.id;
     ```

     JSON and table parity. A row is an event whose live section rows do not match the sections in its old JSON column (rows where a name is repeated in the JSON can be ignored):

     ```sql
     SELECT t.id FROM tournaments t WHERE (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tournament_sections s WHERE s.tournament_id = t.id AND s.archived_at IS NULL) <> (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT json_extract(e.value,'$.name')) + COUNT(DISTINCT CASE e.type WHEN 'text' THEN e.value END) FROM json_each(CASE WHEN json_valid(t.sections) AND json_type(t.sections)='array' THEN t.sections END) e WHERE COALESCE(CASE e.type WHEN 'text' THEN e.value WHEN 'object' THEN json_extract(e.value,'$.name') END,'') <> '');
     ```
- **Merging checkpoint B** (done as recorded above; the notes below stay for the record and for a rollback).
  - Merge when no event is running and no director is editing.
  - The 0054 window: from the moment the new Pages build goes live until 0054 is on production, event creation (the wizard and a club rep) and every save on an LCA-run event answer 500. That covers Details, Registration pricing and visibility, Schedule, Start or Finish event, US Chess upload settings and the admin page's club change. On the real database, club-run events may fail too. Nothing is half-written, because each save is one batch. Player registration, family entry, walk-ins, waitlist offers, check-in and the public pages keep working.
  - Watch `migrate-db.yml` apply 0054 and re-run it at once if it fails. The GitHub Actions Cloudflare token and account ID were replaced on Friday, October 9, 2026 and checked at 2:25 PM UTC with a manual run on `main` (run 37944035297): it reached `lca-db` and found no migrations to apply.
  - Club events created after 0054 is applied but before the new code is live get `requires_lca_membership` 1. After the deploy run `SELECT id, name FROM tournaments WHERE club_id IS NOT NULL AND requires_lca_membership = 1;`. If it returns rows, run 0054's UPDATE again. That is safe while the switch is off and no club has turned it on.
  - Stale tabs keep the old site code until reloaded. An old setup tab still shows the member-discount box; a save from it is accepted and the value is ignored.
  - Rollback, part 1: run `git revert -m 1 <merge sha>` on `main` and push; Pages redeploys the old code (4f0882f). Do not drop `requires_lca_membership` and do not edit 0054; `migrate-db.yml` finds nothing pending. The old code was run against data the new code wrote, with 0054 applied, and GET, PATCH, POST, registration, family entry, walk-in, registration settings and section move all succeeded.
  - Rollback, part 2: member pricing comes back by itself. Members charged full price while checkpoint B was live are not refunded automatically. While rolled back, club events the old wizard creates get `requires_lca_membership` 1, so run 0054's UPDATE again before rolling forward. Tabs left open on the new code after a revert crash the event page until reloaded. When re-merging, put any migration change in 0055, never in 0054.
- **Before reviewing the preview**, K runs `npm run db:migrate:preview` from the branch (ordering rule 2). Done on Monday, October 12, 2026 from the cloud session: the guard passed, and `lca-db-preview` went from 0042 to 0054 (`migrations list` then reported nothing pending).

### Step 16: server helpers grouped by area

Checkpoint C (steps 16 to 19) moves files and changes nothing else. This step moves the server helpers; `functions/api` stays where it is, because its paths are the URLs.

- **The tools.** `scripts/move-files.ts <map.json> [--dry-run]` (run with `node --no-warnings --experimental-strip-types`, like the other TypeScript scripts) runs `git mv` for each entry and rewrites every specifier that reaches a moved file, in every code file under `src`, `functions`, `workers`, `domain`, `test`, `scripts` and `scanner` and at the root: static imports, export-from, `import()`, `typeof import()`, `vi.mock`, `vi.doMock`, `vi.unmock`, `vi.doUnmock`, `vi.importActual`, `vi.importMock` and `require`, keeping a `?raw` query, a `@/` or `@domain/` alias and the specifier's own form (no extension, the file's extension, `.js` for a `.ts` file, or a folder for its index). A moved file's own relative imports of files that stay are rewritten too. Each specifier is resolved with `ts.resolveModuleName` and the options and paths of `tsconfig.app.json` (`tsconfig.functions.json` has no paths); a `.sql`, `.md`, `.css` or `.json` target is resolved as a path. Every new specifier is resolved again against the layout after the move and must reach the same file. Any relative or alias specifier that cannot be resolved stops the run with exit code 1, before anything is moved or written; `--dry-run` prints the plan and the problems. `scripts/verify-move.ts <map.json>` checks the result against git: every file in the map is a rename (`git diff -M`), and in the moved files and every other changed file each changed line is a specifier line that is the same once its specifiers are blanked out, or a line whose only change is a moved path written out in a comment or string. It lists both kinds line by line, so a reviewer can read them separately.
- **The map,** `scripts/moves/server-areas.json`:
  - `events/`: `tournament-manage`, `prizes`, `champions` and `linkPreview`, beside `sectionsRepo` and `schedulesRepo`, which were already there.
  - `registration/`: `registrationEmails` and `registrationOpenNotify`.
  - `membership/`: `membershipActivation`, `membershipExpiry` and `ratingSnapshots`.
  - `live/swiss/`: the swiss folder whole (`engine`, `matching`, `tiebreaks` and their three tests).
  - `admin/`: `audit`, `campaigns`, `tickets`, `permissions` and `members`.
  - At the root, unchanged: the platform files (`auth`, `response`, `json`, `csv`, `email`, `emailLayout`, `site`, `supabase`, `stripe`, `uscf`, `posts`), the four one-line re-exports of `domain/` (`pricing`, `sectionRules`, `regions`, `time`), and `family.ts` (see Deviations). `scan/` is untouched, so the scanner's own checks are not set off.
- **The run.** `--dry-run` on the map: 15 entries (20 files), 90 specifiers in 63 files, 462 files searched, 0 that could not be resolved. Applied, then `verify-move` against the commit before the move, with `--ignore REDESIGN_STATUS.md` because this record changes in the same commit: 20 renames (19 specifier lines and 17 path mentions inside them), 59 other files (71 specifier lines and 13 path mentions), nothing else. The path mentions are the moved files' header comments, comments that name a moved file (`tournament-manage.ts`, `generate-pairings.ts`, `support.ts`, the daily-emails worker, `src/lib/api.ts`, `src/pages/AdminEmailPage.tsx` and `domain/contracts/events.ts`), the swiss include in `vitest.config.ts`, and the path strings in three tests (see Deviations).
- **Bundles.** `npx wrangler pages functions build` (with `WRANGLER_SEND_METRICS=false`, no account needed) compiles before and after; the two bundles differ only in module names and path comments. From `workers/daily-emails`, `npx wrangler deploy --dry-run` needs no account either: 31.96 KiB before and 31.99 KiB after, and the bundles differ only in three path comments. `tsc -p workers/daily-emails/tsconfig.json` passes with its include unchanged.
- **The daily-emails worker redeploys when checkpoint C merges.** `.github/workflows/deploy-worker.yml` runs on any change under `functions/utils/**` on `main`, so the merge deploys the worker again. Its code is the same; only the paths it imports from changed (`campaigns`, `membershipExpiry` and `ratingSnapshots`).
- **Merge order.** Ordering rule 3: no other branch touches `functions/utils` while checkpoint C is open.
- **The brief's helper paths are now out of date.** `REDESIGN_SPEC.md` and `DESIGN_REPLAN_phase1.md` were written before this step and still name the old paths, for example `functions/utils/registrationEmails.ts`, `functions/utils/swiss/`, `tournament-manage.ts`, `audit.ts`, `members.ts` and `membershipActivation.ts`. See Decisions, "Server helper paths".
- Checks: `npm run lint` is 34 errors and 4 warnings before and after, with the same messages; `functions/utils/live/swiss/matching.ts` keeps its two existing errors and `functions/api/support.ts` its one (see Deviations), and the new scripts and test lint clean. `npm run build`, `npm run check:bundle` (no zod in 115 files), `npm run typecheck:functions` and `npm run test:all` pass: unit 1467 in 68 files (1457 in 67 before), integration 790 in 58 files (unchanged). `scanner:check` was not run; nothing under `scanner/` or `functions/utils/scan/` changed.

### Step 17: site library grouped by area

The second of checkpoint C's moves. Nine site library files move from `src/lib` into area folders under `src/areas`; nothing else changes.

Applied with the same script as step 16 and no change to it: its search already covers `.tsx` files and the tests under `test/unit`, and none of the cases it had no test for occurs here. No test pins the path of a moved file.
- **`verify-move`** against the commit before the move, with `--ignore REDESIGN_STATUS.md --ignore docs/redesign/REDESIGN_SPEC.md` because both records change in the same commit: 9 renames (no specifier lines and 7 path mentions inside them), 28 other files (33 specifier lines and 1 path mention), nothing else. The map and the eight `index.ts` files are new and listed as untracked.
- **The area folders.** Each of `events`, `registration`, `households`, `live`, `membership`, `clubs`, `admin` and `governance` has an `index.ts`. `events`, `clubs` and `admin` re-export their own modules with `export *` (no export name repeats inside an area). `clubs/index.ts` also re-exports `@domain/clubs/regions` and `households/index.ts` re-exports `@domain/households/family`, the same modules `src/lib/regions.ts` and `src/lib/family.ts` re-export, so area code has one place to import from. No `index.ts` imports another area or `src/lib`. `registration`, `live`, `membership` and `governance` hold only a one-line comment saying what will live there and `export {}`, so the folders exist for steps 18 and 19. Nothing imports the `index.ts` files yet; every importer names the module itself.

The names:

### Step 18: API client split by area

The third of checkpoint C's moves. The site's API client, `src/lib/api.ts` (2176 lines), is split into one module per area plus the shared request helpers; `src/lib/api.ts` becomes a barrel, so every importer of `@/lib/api` sees the same names and no importer changed.

- **The tool.** `scripts/split-api.ts` (run with `node --no-warnings --experimental-strip-types`, like the other scripts) parses `src/lib/api.ts` with the TypeScript compiler and moves each top-level declaration by its source text, with the comments above it, to the module the map names, keeping the declarations' order. Each module gets a header comment and the imports its declarations use: the original imports (`@/lib/supabase` in `http.ts`, the `RawScan` type in `live`, the five contract types in `events` with the comment above that import), `authHeaders` and `handleResponse` from `@/lib/http`, and with `import type` the types another area owns, from that area's `api.ts`. It stops, writing nothing, when the file declares a name the map lacks or the map names one the file lacks, on a statement kind it does not move, a comment that would be left behind, a private helper of one area used by another, an import of an area from `http.ts`, or a cycle between areas. `--dry-run` prints the counts per module and the imports each gets. `--check` reads the result: the barrel holds only export-from lines and exports exactly the names in `test/unit/fixtures/api-exports.json`, each through one line; each of those names is exported by one module only; each module declares exactly the names the map gives it; no area module imports `src/lib/api.ts`, `http.ts` imports no area and no area `index.ts` re-exports `api.ts`. `--compare <rev>` adds the byte check against `src/lib/api.ts` as it was at that commit.
- **The map,** `scripts/moves/api-split.json`: a JSON object of name to target, in the order of the old file, naming all 185 top-level declarations (182 exports and the private helpers `authHeaders`, `handleResponse` and `exportQuery`). Summarised under Decisions, "API client by area".
- **The fixtures, taken first.** From the unsplit file, before anything else changed: `test/unit/fixtures/api-exports.json`, the 182 exported names (117 functions, the class `ApiError`, 54 interfaces and 10 type aliases), read from the syntax and from the TypeScript checker, which agreed; and `test/unit/fixtures/api-fetch-paths.json`, the 77 distinct paths route-audit extracts (the regex matches 122 times on 104 lines).
- **The run.** `--dry-run`: 185 declarations into 9 modules, 0 problems. Applied, then `--check --compare bb92e90`: 182 names through the barrel, the same set as the snapshot, each in one module; 185 declarations (64,142 characters) equal byte for byte; the rest of the old file is its 3 import statements and 1 newline. Running the script again on the old file in an empty folder writes the same ten files byte for byte.
  - `src/lib/http.ts`: 3 (`authHeaders`, `ApiError`, `handleResponse`).
  - `src/areas/events/api.ts`: 49. `registration`: 14. `households`: 5. `live`: 8. `membership`: 19, plus the private `exportQuery`. `clubs`: 20. `admin`: 34. `governance`: 32.
- **The byte check, a second way.** Separately from the script: each module's text after its last import line, cut into statements, put back in the old order and joined, equals the old file after its first 7 lines (the header and the imports) once blank lines are left out of both (1,962 lines, no difference). The only differences are blank lines between declarations: two blank lines become one, a line holding only a space becomes empty, and `AnnouncementTone` and `AnnouncementSize`, which had no blank line between them, now have one.
- **`http.ts` keeps its helpers' text.** `authHeaders` and `handleResponse` were private; the area modules need them, so `http.ts` ends with `export { authHeaders, handleResponse }` rather than gaining an `export` keyword on each. The barrel re-exports only `ApiError` from it (`export { ApiError } from '@/lib/http'`), so the two helpers do not become new names on `@/lib/api`.
- **The barrel.** `src/lib/api.ts` is a header comment, `export { ApiError } from '@/lib/http'` and `export * from` each of the eight area modules. The three tests that `vi.mock('@/lib/api')` spread the original module first (`tournament-manage-membership-switch`, `tournament-manage-no-member-discount` and `tournament-pages-pricing`) and pass unchanged, as do `api-section-bodies.test.ts` (which imports `@/lib/api` after mocking `@/lib/supabase`; `http.ts` imports the same module) and the tests that import types or functions from `../../src/lib/api`.
- **Route audit.** `test/unit/route-audit.test.ts` scans `src/lib/api.ts`, `src/lib/http.ts` and every `src/areas/*/api.ts` (it checks it found ten files), keeps the more-than-20 guard over the union, and compares the union with the fixture. Each of the 77 paths still has a serving file.
- **Build.** `npm run build` before and after, with the hash stripped from each name in `dist/assets` (`sed -E 's/-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8}\.(js|mjs|css|woff2?|png|svg|jpg|webp)$/.\1/'`): the same 115 names. Vite transforms 2413 modules instead of 2404 (the nine new files). Unlike step 17 the hashes are not all the same: 46 names change their hash (the main chunk `index`, the shared chunks `label`, `viewOnly` and one `dist` chunk, and 42 page and component chunks that import the main chunk), because the main chunk's module order changed; every file is the same size to within 0.01 kB (the main chunk is 504.75 kB before and after). The build output is otherwise the same, line for line: no circular-import warning before or after, only the existing notice about chunks over 500 kB. The area modules all land in the main chunk with the barrel, as `api.ts` did (a path from each area module, such as `/api/scan` or `/api/board/my-seats`, is found in `index` and in no other file), so no new shared chunk appears.
- **Merge order.** Ordering rule 3 covers `src/lib/api.ts` and the new modules as well: a branch that adds a call to `src/lib/api.ts` while checkpoint C is open conflicts with the barrel; add it to the area's `api.ts` instead.
- **Tests.** Four new unit files and two changed ones. `test/unit/api-exports.test.ts` (9): the exported names of `@/lib/api` equal the snapshot and each is exported by one module. `test/unit/api-split-modules.test.ts` (25): the committed map against the real modules and the placement rules (the helpers and `ApiError` in `http`, the contract-derived types in `events`, the scan call in `live`, `exportQuery` private to `membership`); the import direction (nothing imports the barrel, `http.ts` imports no area, area-to-area imports are type-only with no cycle, the area `index.ts` files untouched); the modules hold declarations only; calls through the barrel send the same path, sign-in header and `ApiError` as before; and a vite bundle of the barrel has no circular-import warning and no zod. `test/unit/api-split-tool.test.ts` (38): `scripts/split-api.ts` on a small unsplit client in a temporary folder (byte-for-byte moves with their comments, the imports it writes, every case that stops the run, `--dry-run`, the real run, `--check` and `--compare` passing and failing), and `--check` on the committed map and the real tree. `test/unit/redesign-status-k3c.test.ts` (9): the K3c row, the placement decision, this block's wording and the step 18 sentence in `REDESIGN_SPEC.md`. `test/unit/route-audit.test.ts` gains 2 cases (described above), and `test/unit/client-sections-audit.test.ts` keeps its 13 cases, now reading `src/areas/events/api.ts` (see Deviations).
- Checks: `npm run lint` is 34 errors and 4 warnings before and after, with the same messages on the same lines; the new modules, the script and the tests lint clean. `npm run build`, `npm run check:bundle` (no zod in 115 files), `npm run typecheck:functions`, `tsc -p tsconfig.app.json --noEmit` and `npm run test:all` pass: unit 1550 in 72 files (1467 in 68 before: 81 tests in the four new files above and 2 new cases in route-audit), integration 790 in 58 files (unchanged). `scanner:check` was not run; nothing under `scanner/`, `src/lib/scanner` or `functions/utils/scan/` changed.

### Step 19: components grouped by area, with boundary rules

The last of checkpoint C's moves. The domain components move from `src/components` into a `components` folder inside their area, and three lint rules keep the areas and the pages apart from here on.

- **The map,** `scripts/moves/client-components.json` (18 entries, 34 files):
  - `src/areas/events/components/`: `AgendaList`, `GradeConfirm`, `MiniCalendar`, `PreviewBanner`, `PrizeWinners`, `PrizesEditor`, `SectionRulesEditor` and `UsChessUploadPanel`, from `src/components/tournaments`.
  - `src/areas/live/components/`: `Crosstable`, `StandingsTable` and `ResultsEntry`, the rest of `src/components/tournaments`, which is now empty and gone.
  - `src/areas/households/components/`: the `family` folder whole (`FamilyCard` and `FamilyRegistrationPanel`).
  - `src/areas/admin/components/`: the `admin` folder whole (11 files) and `src/components/AdminAnnouncementPanel.tsx`.

Every moved file keeps its name. The area `index.ts` files do not change (see Decisions, "Components by area").
- **The run.** `--dry-run` on the map: 18 moves (34 files), 59 specifiers in 27 files, 486 files searched, 0 that could not be resolved. Applied with the same script as steps 16 and 17 and no change to it. Every specifier it rewrote is an `@/` alias: 57 static imports (in 18 pages and 8 of the moved components) and 2 dynamic `import()` calls, the lazy `RichTextEditor` in `GovernanceDocuments` and the `FamilyRegistrationPanel` import in `test/unit/tournament-pages-pricing.test.tsx`. `src/App.tsx` imports only shared pieces (`auth/`, `layout/`, `AnnouncementBanner`, `ImpersonationBanner`) and lazy-loads pages, so it did not change, and no shared component imports a moved one.
- **Path mentions fixed by hand.** The header comment naming its own old path in 31 of the 34 moved files (`AdminAnnouncementPanel`, `AgendaList` and `MiniCalendar` have none), and five pinned paths in two tests: four lines of `test/unit/client-sections-audit.test.ts` (`FamilyRegistrationPanel`, `TournamentWizard`, `SectionRulesEditor` and `PrizesEditor`) and one of `test/unit/no-member-price-copy.test.ts` (`FamilyRegistrationPanel`). No test mocked a component path: `vi.mock` and `vi.doMock` of `@/components/` matched nothing under `test/unit` before the move.
